                                                          Answer: (a)

                                                                    where the President is a nominal executive
                                                                    and the real powers are exercised by the
                                                                    Council of Ministers headed by the Prime
                                                                    Minister.

                                                                    not rule.
                                                               • The President acts on the aid and advice of
                                                                    the Council of Ministers, and cannot act
                                                                    independently in most matters.

                                                       Q4. Council of Ministers of the Union of India is
                                                       responsible to the
                                                       (a) Parliament
                                                       (b) President
                                                       (c) Prime Minister
                                                       (d) Chief Justice
                                                       Answer: (a)
                                                       Explanation:
                                                            • Article 75(3) of the Constitution states that
                                                                the Council of Ministers shall be collectively
                                                                responsible to the Lok Sabha.
                                                            • This means all ministers stand or fall
                                                                together; if a no-confidence motion is passed
                                                                in the Lok Sabha, the entire council must
                                                                resign.
                                                            • The principle ensures parliamentary
                                                                accountability and is a key feature of the
                                                                parliamentary system of government.

Q5. The greatest hallmark of personal liberty is                         legal framework for inter-religious
(a) Mandamus                                                             and civil marriages.
(b) Certiorari                                            Hence, option (b) is correct
(c) Quo Warranto
(d) Habeas Corpus
Answer: (d)
Explanation:
     • The writ of Habeas Corpus literally means “to
         have the body”.
     • It is issued by the courts to produce a person
         who has been detained unlawfully, ensuring
         protection of personal liberty.
     • It can be filed by any person on behalf of the
         detainee, making it a powerful tool against
         arbitrary detention.
     • Other writs like Mandamus, Certiorari, and
         Quo Warranto serve different administrative
         or judicial control functions.
     • Habeas Corpus is considered the bulwark of
         individual freedom in a democracy.
Hence, option (d) is correct

the Panchayati Raj?                                                 refers to offences under regular judicial
(a) Tripura and Sikkim                                              procedure, not private complaints.

Answer: (c)
Explanation:
     • Meghalaya and Nagaland are not covered by
         the 73rd Constitutional Amendment Act,
         1992, which provides for the constitutional
         status of Panchayati Raj Institutions.
     • This exclusion is due to their distinct tribal
         traditions, customary laws, and existing local
         governance systems protected under Sixth
         Schedule and Article 371A.

Q11. In the context of India, which one of the
following is the correct relationship between Rights
and Duties?
(a) Rights are correlative with Duties.
(b) Rights are personal and hence independent of
society and Duties.

advancement of the personality of the citizen.

the State.

Q15. With reference to the Indian polity, consider
the following statements
      1. The Governor is not answerable to any court
           for official acts.
      2. No criminal proceedings shall be instituted or
           continued against the Governor during his
           term.
      3. Members of a State Legislature are immune
           from court proceedings for anything said
           within the House.
Which of the statements given above are correct?
(a) I and II only
(b) II and III only
(c) I and III only
(d) I, II and III
Answer: (d) I, II and III
Explanation:
      • Statement I (Correct): Article 361(1) –
           Governor’s immunity from legal action.
      • Statement II (Correct): Article 361(2) –
           Absolute immunity from criminal
           proceedings.
      • Statement III (Correct): Article 194(2) –
           Freedom of speech in Legislature with legal
           immunity.
           Hence option d correct.
